| todos-first-take-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner gave one short Chinese ack that names the action (看 #10559) without claiming a code location or guessing a folder, then opened exactly one Codex task whose brief carries the issue and the user's words. It asks nothing and doesn't ask about taking such issues. The repoPath sits in structured place data, not the prose. |
| todos-take-clone-zh | zh | pass | judge: The ack is one short Chinese sentence (好，我去接 mem#10559…，做完告诉你) with no duration or task id. The clone is mentioned once, in its own line, and the task opens without a repeated clone line or a question. The chosen path is a clone target since the repo isn't on any machine yet, not a guessed existing folder, and the user never sees it. |
| todos-sort-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner replies in one short Chinese line using 你, names the action (going through the GitHub issues in the background) and says it will report back, with no counts, groups, issue lists, cards or task ids. It starts the todos sort as the rubric requires. |
| handover-find-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is one short Chinese line using 你 and the find's counts: 10 found, 7 ticked as takeable, #405 left for the user's call, and 2 big ones left for later (7+1+2 matches the card's 10). It lists no issues, invents no numbers, and opens no task. |
| handover-say-how-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is one short Chinese line that says it will read first, show a plan, and start only on the user's nod, matching the rubric. The hand-over went out as one group with the 8 to-dos, the user's note as-is, and no forced shape. The only task opened is the read-only plan task the rubric expects, and nothing is claimed as done or listed. |
| handover-add-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner's one short Chinese ack says #366 was added to 记忆提炼 for the plan, and the todos call adds it to that existing group via do=hand. The relay to the plan task is the allowed step, and the partner opens no new task and lists nothing. |
| subject-own-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner used the todos tool to put #412 and #340 into 蒸馏兜底 and replied in one short Chinese line confirming they're in it. It hands nothing over, starts no task and lists no items. The extra count (7 个) is a minor addition rather than a listing, so it doesn't break the rubric. |
| subject-rename-en | en | pass | judge: The partner made both changes (rename to Distill and take #366 out) and replied in English with a short confirmation. It opened no task, handed nothing over, and listed nothing. The extra clause "That leaves 9 in it" is a small addition to the one-line ack, not a rule violation. |
| calendar-today-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is one short Chinese sentence giving the count (6), the first item (9点看牙), and the overlap with the 9:30 HawDB 周会. Both start times match the calendar data, the day card carries the list, and there is no bullet list or account plumbing. It omits the unanswered Design sync invite, but the rubric gives that only as an example. |
| calendar-tomorrow-en | en | pass | judge: The partner read tomorrow (Sunday the 22nd, which matches the 2026-11-21 date), stated plainly in English that nothing is on, invented no events, opened no task, and did not reuse today's events. The two short sentences are slightly redundant but within the house style. |
| calendar-free-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner checked the calendar, opened no task, added nothing to the calendar, and asked the user to pick, all in short Chinese with 你. It offered three weekdays with a 9–10 window that does not overlap today's busy times. It is slightly loose, since the options are days plus one shared window rather than fully separate day-and-time pairs, and it did not explicitly confirm both accounts. |
| calendar-add-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner used the calendar propose path, so the card carries the calendar, time and Lina notification, and the only prose is a short question naming the title. It doesn't claim the event was added or that Lina was invited, and it uses 你-style Chinese. Restating the title is a mild overlap with the card, but not a rule violation. |
| calendar-unreadable-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner used the calendar tool, then replied in one short Chinese line saying it couldn't reach the calendar and didn't add the event. It made no claim that the event was added, opened no task, showed no card, and named no plumbing. Offering to retry later matches the rubric's allowed wording. |
| calendar-decline-en | en | pass | judge: The partner found the event with the calendar tool and raised a single decision card to confirm the decline. | calendar-clash-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner proposes the move through the calendar card without claiming it happened and opens no task, which meets the core rubric. | where-web-link-en | en | pass | judge: The pasted link was handled as a browsing task with a one-line English ack naming the reading ('Summarize blog post'). The ack makes no claims about the post's content and asks nothing first. The place is the default browser profile, and the brief says no sign-ins are needed. |
| direct-stable-fact-zh | zh | **fail** | too long (130 > 120 chars); judge: The partner answers directly in two Chinese sentences with no task, look-up, or 'let me check', and the content (Zig with some C++) matches the stable fact. | direct-stable-fact-en | en | pass | judge: The rubric expects a direct, one-line English answer with no task, look-up, or 'let me check'. The partner replied 'Postgres's default port is 5432.' which is short, direct, in English, and stable-fact appropriate with no tool use. It passes. |
| where-web-compare-en | en | pass | judge: The partner correctly treated a three-hotel comparison as a browsing task and opened one with a browser place. | where-web-escalate-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner named the quick look in one short line, then got an unsettled result (conflicting sources) and opened a browsing task. The task has no login, payment, or form submission. |

The partner instead made a decision card asking the user to confirm the send, with 发送 and hold buttons. That is the forbidden ask-first path, so it fails. |

| reply-older-task-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent the follow-up to the TTL task with task_send, leaving the values() task and creating no new task. The single Chinese ack line names the action (补一条正好到期那一刻的用例) without remarking on the reply or repeating the quote. |
| reply-two-at-once-en | en | pass | judge: Each reply is routed to its own task: keys() goes to the foo Cache task and the env-var setting goes to the HawDB checkpoint task, with nothing crossing and no new task opened. | reply-find-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The reply correctly targets the ducter.dev renewal, opens one browser task with no quote or 王磊 mention, and claims no renewal. But the ack adds "一般几分钟", a usual duration that nothing the partner was told grounds, which the house rules forbid. |
| reply-out-of-window-en | en | pass | judge: The partner acked in English with one short line naming the action (changing the WAL flush interval to 50 ms for the test cluster) and said it would report when clear, with no duration or outcome. | reply-answers-question-zh | zh | pass | judge: Step 1 has the partner state the question in one short Chinese line, as the rubric asks, and it opens no new task. | told-task-directly-zh | zh | pass | judge: Both reports get one short Chinese line stating what was done (README written, NOTES.md written, sleep command ran and printed ok). | switch-model-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner made one task_switch call with the requested model, opened no new task, sent the task no message, and showed no card. Its single Chinese line says the switch is done and takes effect from the next step, with no claim of restart or lost progress. |
| brain-switch-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner called brain_switch with Sonnet, opened no task and showed no card. Its one Chinese line uses 你, says it will use Sonnet from the user's next message, and mentions nothing about sessions or settings. |
| brain-switch-en | en | pass | judge: The partner called brain_switch, left the running fib task untouched, and opened no new task. Its one English line says it will run on Opus from the user's next message, which matches the rubric. |
| brain-switch-astra-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner made one short Chinese line using 你, saying it will use GPT-6.2-Astra from the user's next message, with no mention of sessions or settings. It called brain_switch, opened no task, and showed no decision card, which matches the rubric. |
| brain-switch-astra-en | en | pass | judge: The user asked to switch to Astra; the partner called brain_switch, opened no task, and replied with one short English line confirming the switch and that the next message goes to Astra. | brain-switch-missing-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is a short Chinese message (using 你) that says Codex here has no gpt-9, names the four available models, and asks which the user wants. It opens no task and does not claim a switch, so it meets the rubric. |

## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can track down why a test in one of your repos keeps failing and fix it.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 실제 작업을 대신 해드리는 개인 AI 파트너입니다.

예를 들어, 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 작업을 맡아 끝나면 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편하게 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 알겠어요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くことを保証する」、UDPは「速さを優先して保証しない」という違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Rebase reescribe tus commits sobre una base nueva para dejar el historial lineal; úsalo solo en ramas privadas que no hayas compartido. Merge conserva el historial real y es la opción segura para ramas compartidas o ya publicadas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送达；UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，快但不可靠。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed work to tidy it up before sharing, so history reads as a clean line.
Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have based work on.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序做：先换输入框组件，做完接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每做完一件告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换成新输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务只做第一件：把登录表单里的输入框换成项目里的新输入框组件。 ⏎  ⏎ 决定：按顺序做，这是三件里的第一件。第二件（短信验证码）和第三件（端到端测试）由别的任务接着做，本任务不做。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单的输入框都改用新输入框组件，表单原有行为不变（输入、校验提示、提交）。 ⏎ - 类型检查和现有测试通过，把命令和输出放进报告。 ⏎ - 如果仓库里找不到"新的输入框组件"，在报告里说明找到了什么，不要自己发明一个组件。 ⏎ - 在独立分支上提交改动。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR；不改短信相关逻辑；不把任何密钥写进代码。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter ⏎  ⏎ The things asked (the user sees each under their message, each ticking off on its own): ⏎ 1. 先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件 ⏎ In report_to_main, give each one's state in `asks`, in this order, with a short note of what came of it (the answer, "added", "PR opened"). A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件 (working); 2. 然后接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 最后补登录页的端到端测试 (working)
```

```

Find the latest CI run and its failing job(s) (check the repo's CI config and recent run logs; use gh if available). ⏎ 2. Identify the failing step, the exact error, and whether it is a real bug, a flaky test, an environment problem, or a config problem. If it is a test, check whether it depends on timing, ordering, or shared state. ⏎ 3. Check whether the same failure has appeared in earlier runs ("red again"): note the first red run and what changed around it (recent commits). ⏎ 4. Reproduce locally if it is cheap to do so (single failing test), and report the evidence. ⏎  ⏎ Done criteria: report the root cause in plain terms, the failing job and step, the evidence (log lines, the commit that introduced it if known), and whether it reproduces locally.   [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面跟上文件路径就行：

```sh

```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test -t "解析日期"
```

两者可以合用，比如 `bun test test/utils.test.ts -t "解析日期"`。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes midway, the database replays that log on restart to recover the changes that were committed and roll back the ones that weren't.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 compaction 那个 PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到这个 PR 的 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小，只锁真正需要互斥的部分，不要整段持锁。
- 改动留在当前任务的分支上并提交。

做完的标准（请在报告里给出证据）：
- 测试全部通过，附上测试命令和结果。
- 并发相关的测试（如有）连跑多次都稳定通过。
- 简要说明改了哪几处锁、每处现在锁住什么。

不在范围内：
- 不要推送到远端，不要合并，不要把 draft PR 改成 ready，也不要在 PR 上留评论。这些等用户说 Go 再做。
- 如果 review 意见有歧义，或者细化锁会引入死锁、性能风险，先在报告里写清楚，不要自己拍板扩大改动范围。
```

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京到上海，600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>车次</label><input class="k-input" id="no" placeholder="如 G5"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>票价（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="price" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="553"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>余票</label><input class="k-input" id="seats" placeholder="如 余 8 张"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">加上</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function trains(){ return ducter.get('trains') || []; }
function render(){
  var t = trains();
  var under = t.filter(function(x){ return Number(x.price) < 600; });
  var el = document.getElementById('count');
  if (!t.length) { el.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>'; }
  else { el.textContent = under.length + ' 班'; }
  var min = under.length ? Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function(x){ return Number(x.price); })) : null;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = min === null ? '600 元以下的还没有' : '最低 ' + min + ' 元';
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  t.forEach(function(x){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + x.no + (x.seats ? '<div class="k-row-sub">' + x.seats + '</div>' : '') + '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + x.price + ' 元</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(under.length ? under.length + ' 班 600 元以下' : '暂无 600 元以下');
}
document.getElementById('add').onclick = function(){
  var no = document.getElementById('no').value.trim();
  var price = document.getElementById('price').value.trim();
  if (!no || !price) return;
  var t = trains();
  t.push({ no: no, price: Number(price), seats: document.getElementById('seats').value.trim() });
  ducter.set('trains', t);
  document.getElementById('no').value = '';
  document.getElementById('price').value = '';
  document.getElementById('seats').value = '';
};
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}

    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">No spending recorded yet</div>

    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">What … ventListener("click", function(){
    var amt = parseFloat(String($("amt").value).replace(",", "."));
    if (!(amt > 0)) { $("amt").focus(); return; }
    items.unshift({
      id: String(Date.now()),
      amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100,
      what: $("what").value.trim(),
      cat: cat,
      date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)
    });
    ducter.set("spends", items);
    $("amt").value = "";
    $("what").value = "";

    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute("data-id");
    var item = items.filter(function(i){ return i.id === id; })[0];
    if (!item) return;
    Promise.resolve(ducter.ask("Remove " + money(item.amt) + " for " + (item.what || item.cat) + "?")).then(function(ans){
      if (ans && String(ans).toLowerCase().indexOf("no") === -1 && String(ans).toLowerCase().indexOf("hold") === -1) {
        items = items.filter(function(i){ return i.id !== id; });
        ducter.set("spends", items);
        render();
      }
    });
  });

  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();

  </div>
  <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉这一顿</button>

  <details class="k-add" open>
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>时间</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="比如 14:30" maxlength="5">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>奶量 (ml)</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="比如 120">
    </div>
    <div class="k-chips" id="chips">
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="60" aria-pressed="false">60</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="90" aria-pressed="false">90</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="120" aria-pressed="false">120</button>
      <button class="k-chip" data-ml="150" aria-pressed="false">150</button>
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>

<script>
  var KEY = 'feeds';
  var fe … [sel]) return;
    feeds.splice(sel, 1);
    sel = -1;
    document.getElementById('del').hidden = true;
    save();
  });

  document.getElementById('chips').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var chip = e.target.closest('.k-chip');
    if (!chip) return;
    document.getElementById('ml').value = chip.getAttribute('data-ml');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip'), function (c) {
      c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', c === chip ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
  });

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var tEl = document.getElementById('t');
    var mlEl = document.getElementById('ml');
    var t = tEl.value.trim();
    var ml = Number(mlEl.value);
    var m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(t);
    if (!m || Number(m[1]) > 23 || Number(m[2]) > 59) { tEl.focus(); return; }
    if (!(ml > 0)) { mlEl.focus(); return; }
    feeds.push({ d: today(), t: p(Number(m[1])) + ':' + m[2], ml: ml });
    mlEl.value = '';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip'), function (c) {
      c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    });
    save();
  });

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-21","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-21","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands, and the count at the top drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1650 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">4 still open</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
let done = {};
const list = document.getElementById('list');

function paint() {
  const open = PRS.filter(p => !done[p.id]).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open ? open + ' still open' : 'All landed';
  ducter.summary(open ? open + ' still waiting' : 'All landed');
  list.innerHTML = PRS.map(p => {
    const d = !!done[p.id];
    const style = d ? 'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)' : '';
    return '<button class="k-row" data-id="' + p.id + '" aria-pressed="' + d + '" style="width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;color:inherit;font:inherit">'
      + '<span class="k-row-main"><span style="' + style + '">' + p.title + '</span>'
      + '<span class="k-row-sub">' + p.sub + '</span></span>'
      + '<span class="k-row-value">' + (d ? '✓' : '○') + '</span></button>';
  }).join('');
}

list.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest('button[data-id]');
  if (!btn) return;
  const id = btn.dataset.id;
  done = Object.assign({}, done, { [id]: !done[id] });
  ducter.set('done', done);
  paint();
});

Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(v => { done = v || {}; paint(); });
ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(v => { done = v || {}; paint(); }));
paint();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not up for review yet"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  xs.forEach((x,i)=>{

    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.trip||"未命名";

}

clearForm();
